# Skills · C · part 108

801 entries, cx-profiling to cycle. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cx-profiling**: Query Coralogix continuous-profiling data with the `cx profiling` CLI — find which services use the most CPU or memory, which profile types a service has, what profiles can be filtered by, when usage spiked and which pod or version caused it, and which code paths and functions a service spends its CPU or memory on (flame graphs). Use when the user asks "which services use the most CPU", "top memory consumers", "what is service X spending CPU on", "hot functions", "flame graph for a service", "why is this service using so much CPU", "memory allocations by function", "CPU spike by pod", "which… ([coralogix/cx-cli](https://github.com/coralogix/cx-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/cx-profiling) · ★119)
